SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   142	@Composable
   143	fun CinerificApp() {
   144	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   145	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   146	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   147	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   148	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   149	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   150	    }
   151	    var introView by remember { mutableStateOf<CinerificIntroView?>(null) }
   152	    var welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   153	
   154	    fun selectProfile(profile: CinerificProfile) {
   155	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis) return
   156	        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   157	        signedInProfile = profile
   158	        signInSessionId += 1
   159	        showHome = true
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    BackHandler(enabled = !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isNotBlank()) {
   163	        introView?.handleSystemBack()
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    if (showHome) {
   167	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   168	            CinerificMainExperience(
   169	                signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   170	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   171	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   172	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   173	                onSignOut = {
   174	                    welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 500L
   175	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   176	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   177	                    showHome = false
   178	                }
   179	            )
   180	        }
   181	    } else {
   182	        AndroidView(
   183	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   184	            factory = { context ->
   185	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   186	                    introView = this
   187	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   188	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   189	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   190	                        }
   191	                    }
   192	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   193	                    onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   194	                }
   195	            },
   196	            update = { view ->
   197	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   198	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   199	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   200	                    }
   201	                }
   202	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   203	                view.onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   204	            }
   205	        )
   206	    }
   207	}
   208	
   209	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   210	    Home,
   211	    Movies,
   212	    Shows,
   213	    Favorites,
   214	    Settings,
   215	    ProgramDetails
   216	}
   217	
   218	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   219	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   220	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   221	) {
   222	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   223	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   224	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   225	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   226	}
   227	
   228	@Composable
   229	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   230	    signInSessionId: Int,
   231	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   232	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   233	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   234	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   235	) {
   236	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   237	    var detailOriginDestination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) {
   238	        mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home)
   239	    }
   240	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   241	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   242	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   243	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   244	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   245	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   246	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   247	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   248	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   249	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   250	    var primaryDestinationCanScroll by remember(destination) { mutableStateOf<Boolean?>(null) }
   251	    var portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   252	    var portraitBottomNavHeightPx by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   253	    var showSignOutConfirmation by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   254	    var signOutInProgress by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   255	    val destinationStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
   256	    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
   257	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   258	    val isPortrait = configuration.screenHeightDp > configuration.screenWidthDp
   259	    val usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav = destination in setOf(
   260	        CinerificDestination.Home,
   261	        CinerificDestination.Movies,
   262	        CinerificDestination.Shows,
   263	        CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   264	        CinerificDestination.Settings
   265	    )
   266	    val portraitBottomNavScrollConnection = remember(
   267	        isPortrait,
   268	        destination,
   269	        primaryDestinationCanScroll,
   270	        portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   271	    ) {
   272	        object : NestedScrollConnection {
   273	            override fun onPostScroll(
   274	                consumed: Offset,
   275	                available: Offset,
   276	                source: NestedScrollSource
   277	            ): Offset {
   278	                if (
   279	                    isPortrait &&
   280	                    usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   281	                    primaryDestinationCanScroll == true &&
   282	                    consumed.y != 0f
   283	                ) {
   284	                    portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = (
   285	                        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction +
   286	                            consumed.y * PORTRAIT_BOTTOM_NAV_SCROLL_MULTIPLIER /
   287	                            portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   288	                        ).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   289	                }
   290	                return Offset.Zero
   291	            }
   292	        }
   293	    }
   294	
   295	    LaunchedEffect(destination, isPortrait, primaryDestinationCanScroll) {
   296	        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = if (
   297	            isPortrait &&
   298	            usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   299	            primaryDestinationCanScroll != false
   300	        ) {
   301	            1f
   302	        } else {
   303	            0f
   304	        }
   305	    }
   306	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   307	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   308	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   309	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   310	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   311	            },
   312	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   313	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   314	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   315	            }
   316	        )
   317	    }
   318	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   319	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   320	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   321	        catalogRouteModeName
   322	    ) {
   323	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   324	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   325	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   326	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   327	        )
   328	    }
   329	
   330	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   331	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   332	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   333	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   334	    }
   335	
   336	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   337	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   338	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   339	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   340	        if (
   341	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   342	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   343	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   344	        ) {
   345	            onSignOut()
   346	        }
   347	    }
   348	
   349	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   350	        if (destination != CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails) {
   351	            detailOriginDestination = destination
   352	        }
   353	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   354	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   358	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   359	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   360	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   361	        destination = route.destination
   362	    }
   363	
   364	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   365	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   366	    }
   367	
   368	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   369	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   370	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   371	            return
   372	        }
   373	
   374	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   375	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   376	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   377	            isShow = isShow
   378	        )
   379	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   380	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   381	            return
   382	        }
   383	
   384	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   385	    }
   386	
   387	    BackHandler {
   388	        when {
   389	            signOutInProgress -> Unit
   390	            showSignOutConfirmation -> showSignOutConfirmation = false
   391	            destination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> {
   392	                destination = detailOriginDestination
   393	            }
   394	            destination != CinerificDestination.Home -> {
   395	                clearCatalogRoute()
   396	                destination = CinerificDestination.Home
   397	            }
   398	            else -> showSignOutConfirmation = true
   399	        }
   400	    }
   401	
   402	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   403	        Box(
   404	            modifier = Modifier
   405	                .fillMaxSize()
   406	                .nestedScroll(portraitBottomNavScrollConnection)
   407	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   408	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   409	                        while (true) {
   410	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   411	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   412	                        }
   413	                    }
   414	                }
   415	        ) {
   416	            destinationStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(destination) {
   417	                when (destination) {
   418	                    CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   419	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   420	                        onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   421	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   422	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   423	                    )
   424	                    CinerificDestination.Movies,
   425	                    CinerificDestination.Shows,
   426	                    CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   427	                    CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   428	                        destination = destination,
   429	                        signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   430	                        selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   431	                        onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   432	                        autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   433	                        onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   434	                            autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   435	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   436	                        },
   437	                        onSignOut = onSignOut,
   438	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   439	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   440	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   441	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   442	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   443	                        catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   444	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   445	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   446	                    )
   447	                    CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   448	                        programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   449	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   450	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   451	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   452	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   453	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   454	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   455	                    )
   456	                }
   457	            }
   458	
   459	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   460	                currentDestination = destination,
   461	                portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   462	                onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   463	                    portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   464	                },
   465	                onDestinationSelected = {
   466	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   467	                    destination = it
   468	                },
   469	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   470	            )
   471	
   472	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   473	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   474	                currentDestination = destination,
   475	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   476	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   477	            )
   478	
   479	            if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
   480	                SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   481	                    onConfirm = {
   482	                        showSignOutConfirmation = false
   483	                        signOutInProgress = true
   484	                        scope.launch {
   485	                            delay(SIGN_OUT_LOADING_MS)
   486	                            onSignOut()
   487	                        }
   488	                    },
   489	                    onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
   490	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   491	                )
   492	            }
   493	
   494	            if (signOutInProgress) {
   495	                SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
   496	            }
   497	        }
   498	    }
   499	}

   520	@Composable
   521	private fun SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   522	    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
   523	    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
   524	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   525	) {
   526	    val frameShape = RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)
   527	    val panelShape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
   528	    val frameStrokeWidth = 2.dp
   529	    val frameClearSpace = frameStrokeWidth * (10f / 3f)
   530	    val selectionStrokeAlpha = rememberCinerificSelectionStrokeAlpha()
   531	    val selectionStrokeColor = lerpColor(
   532	        Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   533	        Color(0xFFE7E7E7),
   534	        ((selectionStrokeAlpha - 0.5f) / 0.17f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   535	    )
   536	
   537	    Box(
   538	        modifier = modifier,
   539	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   540	    ) {
   541	        Box(
   542	            modifier = Modifier
   543	                .fillMaxSize()
   544	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
   545	                .clickable(onClick = {})
   546	        )
   547	        Box(
   548	            modifier = Modifier
   549	                .fillMaxWidth(0.84f)
   550	                .widthIn(max = 420.dp)
   551	                .border(
   552	                    frameStrokeWidth,
   553	                    selectionStrokeColor,
   554	                    frameShape
   555	                )
   556	                .padding(frameClearSpace)
   557	        ) {
   558	            Column(
   559	                modifier = Modifier
   560	                    .fillMaxWidth()
   561	                    .clip(panelShape)
   562	                    .background(Color(0xFF252126))
   563	                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 22.dp),
   564	                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
   565	                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
   566	            ) {
   567	                Text(
   568	                    text = "Sign out from\nCinerific?",
   569	                    color = Color.White,
   570	                    fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   571	                    fontSize = 26.sp,
   572	                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
   573	                    lineHeight = 34.sp,
   574	                    letterSpacing = 0.55.sp,
   575	                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   576	                )
   577	                Row(
   578	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
   579	                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
   580	                ) {
   581	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   582	                        label = "Yes",
   583	                        backgroundColor = ColorFrame2Background,
   584	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   585	                        borderColor = Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   586	                        onClick = onConfirm,
   587	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   588	                    )
   589	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   590	                        label = "No",
   591	                        backgroundColor = Color(0xFF3A343C),
   592	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   593	                        borderColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.72f),
   594	                        onClick = onDismiss,
   595	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   596	                    )
   597	                }
   598	            }
   599	        }
   600	    }
   601	}

   632	
   633	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   634	    destinationName: String,
   635	    genreName: String,
   636	    modeName: String
   637	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   638	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   639	
   640	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   641	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   642	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   643	
   644	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   645	        destination = destination,
   646	        genre = genre,
   647	        mode = mode
   648	    )
   649	}
   650	
   651	@Composable
   652	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   653	    requestId: Int,
   654	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   655	    suppressed: Boolean,
   656	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   657	) {
   658	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   659	
   660	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   661	        if (suppressed) {
   662	            visible = false
   663	            return@LaunchedEffect
   664	        }
   665	        if (requestId == 0) {
   666	            visible = false
   667	            return@LaunchedEffect
   668	        }
   669	        visible = true
   670	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   671	        visible = false
   672	    }
   673	
   674	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   675	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   676	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   677	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   678	    )
   679	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   680	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   681	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   682	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   683	    )
   684	
   685	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   686	
   687	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   688	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   689	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   690	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   691	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   692	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   693	        } else {
   694	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   695	        }
   696	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   697	            stageScale = stageScale,
   698	            modifier = Modifier
   699	                .absoluteOffset(
   700	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   701	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   702	                )
   703	                .graphicsLayer {
   704	                    this.alpha = alpha
   705	                    scaleX = promptScale
   706	                    scaleY = promptScale
   707	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   708	                }
   709	        )
   710	    }
   711	}
   712	
   713	@Composable
   714	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   715	    stageScale: Float,
   716	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   717	) {
   718	    Box(
   719	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   720	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   721	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   722	        )
   723	    ) {
   724	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   725	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   726	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   727	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   728	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   729	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   730	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   731	                close()
   732	            }
   733	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   734	            drawRoundRect(
   735	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   736	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   737	                size = Size(
   738	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   739	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   740	                ),
   741	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   742	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   743	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   744	                )
   745	            )
   746	            drawRoundRect(
   747	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   748	                topLeft = Offset(
   749	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   750	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   751	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   752	                ),
   753	                size = Size(
   754	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   755	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   756	                ),
   757	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   758	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   759	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   760	                ),
   761	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   762	            )
   763	        }
   764	        Text(
   765	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   766	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   767	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   768	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   769	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   770	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   771	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   772	            modifier = Modifier
   773	                .absoluteOffset(
   774	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   775	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   776	                )
   777	                .requiredSize(
   778	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   779	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   780	                )
   781	        )
   782	    }
   783	}

   785	@Composable
   786	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   787	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   788	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   789	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   790	        p,
   791	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   792	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   793	    )
   794	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   795	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   796	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   797	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   798	    )
   799	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   800	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   801	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   802	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   803	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   804	
   805	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   806	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   807	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   808	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   809	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   810	
   811	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   812	
   813	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   814	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   815	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   816	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   817	                end = baseBounds,
   818	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   819	            )
   820	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   821	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   822	                end = baseBounds,
   823	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   824	            )
   825	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   826	
   827	            drawFigmaImage(
   828	                image = images.logoSimple,
   829	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   830	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   831	                stageTop = stageTop,
   832	                stageScale = stageScale,
   833	                alpha = logoAlpha
   834	            )
   835	            drawFigmaImage(
   836	                image = images.logoEyes,
   837	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   838	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   839	                stageTop = stageTop,
   840	                stageScale = stageScale,
   841	                alpha = logoAlpha
   842	            )
   843	        }
   844	
   845	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   846	            val y = avatarYOffset
   847	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   848	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   849	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   850	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   851	
   852	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   853	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   854	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   855	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   856	        }
   857	    }
   858	}

   974	@Composable
   975	private fun LogoSlide2Layer(
   976	    scale: Float,
   977	    @DrawableRes logoResId: Int,
   978	    @DrawableRes eyesResId: Int,
   979	    alpha: Float = 1f
   980	) {
   981	    Box(
   982	        modifier = Modifier
   983	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   984	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
   985	    ) {
   986	        LogoDot(x = 427f, y = 516f, scale = scale, active = false)
   987	        LogoDot(x = 507f, y = 516f, scale = scale, active = false)
   988	        LogoDot(x = 587f, y = 516f, scale = scale, active = false)
   989	        LogoDot(x = 667f, y = 516f, scale = scale, active = true)
   990	
   991	        // Visible logo bounds from the Figma instance in the 1194x834 frame.
   992	        FigmaAssetImage(
   993	            x = 222f,
   994	            y = 150f,
   995	            w = 750f,
   996	            h = 535f,
   997	            scale = scale,
   998	            resId = logoResId
   999	        )
  1000	
  1001	        // Eye layer sits on the same logo bounds.

  1003	            x = 222f,
  1004	            y = 150f,
  1005	            w = 750f,
  1006	            h = 535f,
  1007	            scale = scale,
  1008	            resId = eyesResId
  1009	        )
  1010	    }

  1051	
  1052	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
  1053	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1054	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
  1055	    stageLeft: Float,
  1056	    stageTop: Float,
  1057	    stageScale: Float,
  1058	    alpha: Float = 1f,
  1059	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
  1060	) {
  1061	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
  1062	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
  1063	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
  1064	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
  1065	
  1066	    if (clipCircle) {
  1067	        val clip = Path().apply {
  1068	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
  1069	        }
  1070	        clipPath(clip) {
  1071	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1072	        }
  1073	    } else {
  1074	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1075	    }
  1076	}
  1077	
  1078	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
  1079	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1080	    left: Float,
  1081	    top: Float,
  1082	    width: Float,
  1083	    height: Float,
  1084	    alpha: Float
  1085	) {
  1086	    drawImage(
  1087	        image = image,
  1088	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
  1089	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
  1090	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1091	    )
  1092	}

  1094	@Composable
  1095	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
  1096	    scale: Float,
  1097	    entryProgress: Float,
  1098	    finalProgress: Float,
  1099	    alpha: Float
  1100	) {
  1101	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1102	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1103	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1104	        end = baseBounds,
  1105	        amount = entryProgress
  1106	    )
  1107	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1108	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1109	        end = baseBounds,
  1110	        amount = entryProgress
  1111	    )
  1112	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1113	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1114	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1115	
  1116	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1117	        base = baseBounds,
  1118	        target = simpleBounds,
  1119	        scale = scale,
  1120	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
  1121	        alpha = alpha
  1122	    )
  1123	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1124	        base = baseBounds,
  1125	        target = eyesBounds,
  1126	        scale = scale,
  1127	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
  1128	        alpha = alpha
  1129	    )
  1130	}

  1133	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
  1134	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density

  1152	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
  1153	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)

  1164	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,

  1188	@Composable
  1189	private fun SignInPromptText(
  1190	    text: String,
  1191	    x: Float,
  1192	    y: Float,
  1193	    textSize: Float,
  1194	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
  1195	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1196	    scale: Float
  1197	) {
  1198	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
  1199	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
  1200	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
  1201	        else -> Alignment.Center
  1202	    }
  1203	    Box(
  1204	        modifier = Modifier
  1205	            .absoluteOffset(
  1206	                x = figma(x, scale),
  1207	                y = figma(y, scale)
  1208	            )
  1209	            .requiredSize(
  1210	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
  1211	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
  1212	            ),
  1213	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
  1214	    ) {
  1215	        Text(
  1216	            text = text,
  1217	            color = Color.White,
  1218	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1219	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
  1220	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
  1221	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
  1222	            maxLines = 1,
  1223	            softWrap = false,
  1224	            textAlign = textAlign
  1225	        )
  1226	    }
  1227	}

  1266	) {

  1269	        x = 0f,

  1282	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))

  1289	@Composable
  1290	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1291	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1292	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1293	    scale: Float,
  1294	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1295	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1296	) {
  1297	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1298	    Image(
  1299	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1300	        contentDescription = null,
  1301	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1302	        modifier = Modifier
  1303	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1304	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1305	            .graphicsLayer {
  1306	                this.alpha = alpha
  1307	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1308	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1309	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1310	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1311	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1312	            }
  1313	    )
  1314	}
  1315	
  1316	@Composable
  1317	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1318	    x: Float,
  1319	    y: Float,
  1320	    w: Float,
  1321	    h: Float,
  1322	    scale: Float,
  1323	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1324	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1325	) {
  1326	    Image(
  1327	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1328	        contentDescription = null,
  1329	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1330	        modifier = Modifier
  1331	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1332	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1333	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1334	    )
  1335	}

  1337	@Composable
  1338	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1339	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1340	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1341	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1342	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1343	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1344	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1345	
  1346	        Box(
  1347	            modifier = Modifier
  1348	                .fillMaxSize()
  1349	                .background(background)
  1350	        ) {
  1351	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1352	                modifier = Modifier
  1353	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1354	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1355	            ) {
  1356	                content(scale)
  1357	            }
  1358	        }
  1359	    }
  1360	}
  1361	
  1362	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1363	
  1364	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1365	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1366	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1367	    } else {
  1368	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1369	    }
  1370	}
  1371	
  1372	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1373	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1374	}
  1375	
  1376	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1377	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1378	}
  1379	
  1380	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1381	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1382	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1383	        colors = listOf(
  1384	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1385	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1386	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1387	        )
  1388	    )
  1389	}
  1390	
  1391	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1392	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1393	)
  1394	
  1395	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1396	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1397	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1398	}
  1399	
  1400	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1401	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1402	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1403	}
  1404	
  1405	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1406	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1407	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1408	}
  1409	
  1410	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1411	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1412	}
  1413	
  1414	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1415	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1416	}
  1417	
  1418	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1419	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1420	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1421	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1422	    return base + bounce
  1423	}
  1424	
  1425	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1426	
  1427	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1428	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1429	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1430	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1431	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1432	)
  1433	
  1434	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1435	    save = { snapshot ->
  1436	        listOf(
  1437	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1438	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1439	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1440	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1441	            snapshot.emailText,
  1442	            snapshot.monthText,
  1443	            snapshot.dayText,
  1444	            snapshot.yearText,
  1445	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1446	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1447	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1448	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1449	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1450	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex
  1451	        )
  1452	    },
  1453	    restore = { values ->
  1454	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(

  1456	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1457	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1458	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1459	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1460	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1461	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1462	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1463	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1464	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1465	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1466	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1467	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,

   421	    private val mockDropdownScrollOffsets = MockDropdown.values().associateWith { 0 }.toMutableMap()

   439	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   440	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   441	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   442	        return true
   443	    }
   444	
   445	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   446	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   447	
   448	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   449	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   450	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   451	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   452	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   453	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   454	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   455	
   456	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   457	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   458	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   459	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   460	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   461	        pressedMockField = null
   462	        pressedRememberMe = false
   463	        pressedBackChevron = false
   464	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   465	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   466	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   467	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   468	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   469	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   470	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   471	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   472	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   473	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   474	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   475	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   476	        activeComposingText = ""
   477	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   478	        focusedMockField = null
   479	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   480	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   481	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   482	
   483	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   484	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   485	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   486	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   487	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   488	        } else {
   489	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   490	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   491	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   492	        }
   493	
   494	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   495	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   496	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   497	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   498	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   499	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   500	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   501	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   502	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   503	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   504	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   505	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   506	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   507	        ) {
   508	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   509	        } else {
   510	            null
   511	        }
   512	
   513	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   514	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   515	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   516	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   517	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   518	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   519	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   520	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   521	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   522	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   523	        }
   524	
   525	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   526	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   527	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   528	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   529	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   530	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   531	    }

   533	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   534	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   535	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   536	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   537	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   538	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   539	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   540	        pressedMockField = null
   541	        pressedRememberMe = false
   542	        pressedBackChevron = false
   543	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   544	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   545	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   546	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   547	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   548	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   549	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   550	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   551	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   552	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   553	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   554	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   555	    }
