   557	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   558	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   559	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   560	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   561	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   562	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   563	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   564	    }
   565	
   566	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   567	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   568	
   569	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   570	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   571	        }
   572	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   573	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   574	            progress,
   575	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   576	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   577	        )
   578	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   579	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   580	
   581	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   582	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   583	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   584	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   585	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   586	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   587	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   588	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   589	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   590	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   591	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   592	
   593	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   594	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   595	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   596	        }
   597	
   598	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   599	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   600	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   601	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   602	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   603	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   604	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   605	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
   606	                    drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   607	                } else {
   608	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   609	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   610	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   611	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   612	
   613	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   614	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   615	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   616	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   617	                }
   618	            }
   619	        }
   620	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   621	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   622	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   623	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   624	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   625	                    canvas,
   626	                    outgoingFlow,
   627	                    stage.left,
   628	                    stage.top,
   629	                    stage.scale,
   630	                    1f - switchMotion,
   631	                    formFocusMotion,
   632	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   633	                )
   634	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   635	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   636	                        canvas,
   637	                        incomingFlow,
   638	                        stage.left,
   639	                        stage.top,
   640	                        stage.scale,
   641	                        switchMotion,
   642	                        formFocusMotion,
   643	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   644	                    )
   645	                }
   646	            } else {
   647	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   648	            }
   649	            drawBackChevron(
   650	                canvas,
   651	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   652	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   653	                stage.left,
   654	                accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   655	                stage.scale,
   656	                mockMotion
   657	            )
   658	        }
   659	        drawAccountPrompt(
   660	            canvas = canvas,
   661	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   662	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   663	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   664	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   665	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   666	            } else {
   667	                0f
   668	            },
   669	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   670	        )
   671	
   672	        if (
   673	            progress < 1f ||
   674	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   675	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   676	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   677	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   678	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   679	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   680	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   681	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   682	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   683	        ) {
   684	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   685	        }
   686	    }
   687	
   688	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   689	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   690	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   691	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   692	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   693	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   694	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   695	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   696	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   697	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   698	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   699	                        return true
   700	                    }
   701	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   702	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   703	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   704	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   705	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   706	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   707	                    if (
   708	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   709	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   710	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   711	                    ) {
   712	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   713	                        return true
   714	                    }
   715	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   716	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   717	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   718	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   719	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   720	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   721	                    } else {
   722	                        null
   723	                    }
   724	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   725	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   726	                    } else {
   727	                        null
   728	                    }
   729	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   730	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   731	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   732	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   733	                    ) {
   734	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   735	                    } else {
   736	                        null
   737	                    }
   738	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   739	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   740	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   741	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   742	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   743	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   744	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   745	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   746	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   747	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   748	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   749	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   750	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   751	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   752	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   753	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   754	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   755	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   756	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   757	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   758	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   759	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   760	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   761	                    ) {
   762	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   763	                    } else {
   764	                        null
   765	                    }
   766	                    pressedMockField = if (
   767	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   768	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   769	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   770	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   771	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   772	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   773	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   774	                    ) {
   775	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   776	                    } else {
   777	                        null
   778	                    }
   779	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   780	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   781	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   782	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   783	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   784	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   785	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   786	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   787	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   788	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   789	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   790	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   791	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   792	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   793	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   794	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   795	                    return true
   796	                }
   797	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   798	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   799	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   800	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   801	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   802	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   803	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   804	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   805	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   806	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   807	                ) {
   808	                    null
   809	                } else {
   810	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   811	                }
   812	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   813	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   814	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   815	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   816	            }
   817	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   818	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   819	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   820	                        return true
   821	                    }
   822	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   823	                        return true
   824	                    }
   825	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   826	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   827	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   828	                    }
   829	                    return true
   830	                }
   831	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   832	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   833	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   834	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   835	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   836	                    }
   837	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   838	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   839	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   840	                    }
   841	                    return true
   842	                }
   843	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   844	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   845	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   846	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   847	            }
   848	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   849	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   850	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   851	                    pressedMockField = null
   852	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   853	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   854	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   855	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   856	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   857	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   858	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   859	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   860	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   861	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   862	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   863	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   864	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   865	                    return true
   866	                }
   867	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   868	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   869	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   870	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   871	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   872	                        return true
   873	                    }
   874	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   875	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   876	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   877	                        }
   878	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   879	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   880	                        }
   881	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   882	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   883	                        }
   884	                        else -> null
   885	                    }
   886	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   887	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   888	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   889	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   890	                        super.performClick()
   891	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   892	                        return true
   893	                    }
   894	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   895	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   896	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   897	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   898	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   899	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   900	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   901	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   902	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   903	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   904	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   905	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   906	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   907	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   908	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   909	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   910	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   911	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   912	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   913	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   914	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   915	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   916	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   917	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   918	                        pressedOption != null &&
   919	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   920	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   921	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   922	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   923	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   924	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   925	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   926	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   927	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   928	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   929	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   930	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   931	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   932	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   933	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   934	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   935	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   936	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   937	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   938	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   939	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   940	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   941	                        pressedOption == null &&
   942	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   943	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   944	                    pressedMockField = null
   945	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   946	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   947	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   948	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   949	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   950	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   951	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   952	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   953	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   954	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   955	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   956	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   957	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   958	                    super.performClick()
   959	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   960	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   961	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   962	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   963	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   964	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   965	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   966	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   967	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   968	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   969	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   970	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   971	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   972	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   973	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   974	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   975	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   976	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   977	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   978	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   979	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   980	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   981	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   982	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   983	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   984	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   985	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   986	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   987	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   988	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   989	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   990	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   991	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   992	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   993	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   994	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   995	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   996	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   997	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   998	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   999	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1000	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1001	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1002	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1003	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1004	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1005	                        }
  1006	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1007	                    } else {
  1008	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1009	                    }
  1010	                    return true
  1011	                }
  1012	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1013	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1014	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1015	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1016	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1017	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1018	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1019	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1020	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1021	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1022	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1023	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1024	                    swipeDirection != null
  1025	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1026	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1027	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
  1028	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1029	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1030	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1031	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1032	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1033	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1034	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1035	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1036	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1037	                    true
  1038	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1039	                    super.performClick()
  1040	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1041	                    true
  1042	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1043	                    super.performClick()
  1044	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1045	                    true
  1046	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1047	                    super.performClick()
  1048	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1049	                    true
  1050	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1051	                    performClick()
  1052	                    true
  1053	                } else {
  1054	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1055	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1056	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1057	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1058	                    false
  1059	                }
  1060	            }
  1061	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1062	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1063	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1064	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1065	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1066	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1067	                pressedMockField = null
  1068	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1069	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1070	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1071	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1072	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1073	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1074	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1075	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1076	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1077	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1078	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1079	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1080	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1081	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1082	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1083	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1084	                false
  1085	            }
  1086	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1087	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1088	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1089	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1090	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1091	        }
  1092	    }

  1094	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1095	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1096	    }

  1098	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1099	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1100	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1101	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1102	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1103	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1104	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1105	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1106	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1107	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1108	        }
  1109	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1110	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1111	        } else {
  1112	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1113	        }
  1114	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1115	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1116	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1117	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1118	    }

  1120	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1121	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1122	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1123	        }
  1124	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1125	    }

  1127	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1128	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1129	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1130	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1131	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1132	        }
  1133	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1134	    }

  1136	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1137	        super.performClick()
  1138	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1139	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1140	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1141	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1142	        return true
  1143	    }

  1145	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1146	        canvas: Canvas,
  1147	        stageLeft: Float,
  1148	        stageTop: Float,
  1149	        stageScale: Float,
  1150	        progress: Float,
  1151	        alpha: Float,
  1152	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1153	    ) {
  1154	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1155	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1156	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1157	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1158	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1159	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1160	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1161	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1162	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1163	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1164	        )
  1165	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1166	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1167	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1168	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1169	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1170	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1171	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1172	        )
  1173	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1174	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1175	            focusShiftY
  1176	        )
  1177	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1178	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1179	            focusShiftY
  1180	        )
  1181	
  1182	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1183	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1184	            return
  1185	        }
  1186	
  1187	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1188	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1189	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1190	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1191	    }
  1192	
  1193	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1194	        canvas: Canvas,
  1195	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1196	        yOffset: Float,
  1197	        alpha: Float
  1198	    ) {
  1199	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1200	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1201	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1202	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1203	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1204	
  1205	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1206	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1207	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1208	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1209	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1210	            )
  1211	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1212	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1213	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1214	            )
  1215	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1216	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1217	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1218	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1219	            )
  1220	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1221	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1222	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1223	            )
  1224	        } else {
  1225	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1226	        }
  1227	    }
  1228	
  1229	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1230	        canvas: Canvas,
  1231	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1232	        yOffset: Float,
  1233	        alpha: Float,
  1234	        profileIndex: Int,
  1235	        offsetX: Float
  1236	    ) {
  1237	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1238	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1239	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1240	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1241	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1242	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1243	        }
  1244	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1245	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1246	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1247	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1248	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1249	        }
  1250	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1251	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1252	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1253	            canvas,
  1254	            avatarBitmap,
  1255	            Bounds(
  1256	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1257	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1258	                avatarSize,
  1259	                avatarSize
  1260	            ),
  1261	            stage.left,
  1262	            stage.top,
  1263	            stage.scale,
  1264	            alpha
  1265	        )
  1266	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1267	            canvas,
  1268	            nameBitmap,
  1269	            Bounds(
  1270	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1271	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1272	                nameWidth,
  1273	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1274	            ),
  1275	            stage.left,
  1276	            stage.top,
  1277	            stage.scale,
  1278	            alpha
  1279	        )
  1280	    }
  1281	
  1282	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1283	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1284	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1285	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1286	            return
  1287	        }
  1288	
  1289	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1290	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1291	            0f,
  1292	            0f,
  1293	            0f,
  1294	            height.toFloat(),
  1295	            intArrayOf(
  1296	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1297	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1298	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1299	            ),
  1300	            null,
  1301	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1302	        )
  1303	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1304	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1305	
  1306	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1307	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1308	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1309	        }
  1310	    }
  1311	
  1312	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1313	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1314	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1315	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1316	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1317	    }
  1318	
  1319	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1320	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1321	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1322	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1323	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1324	    }
  1325	
  1326	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1327	        canvas: Canvas,
  1328	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1329	        bounds: Bounds,
  1330	        stageLeft: Float,
  1331	        stageTop: Float,
  1332	        stageScale: Float,
  1333	        alpha: Float
  1334	    ) {
  1335	        tempRect.set(
  1336	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1337	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1338	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1339	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1340	        )
  1341	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1342	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1343	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1344	    }
  1345	
  1346	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1347	        canvas: Canvas,
  1348	        stageLeft: Float,
  1349	        stageTop: Float,
  1350	        stageScale: Float,
  1351	        yOffset: Float,
  1352	        alpha: Float
  1353	    ) {
  1354	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1355	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1356	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1357	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1358	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1359	            portraitAmount
  1360	        ) * stageScale
  1361	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1362	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1363	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1364	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1365	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1366	
  1367	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1368	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1369	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1370	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1371	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1372	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1373	            standardCenterY,
  1374	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1375	            portraitAmount
  1376	        )
  1377	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1378	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1379	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1380	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1381	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1382	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1383	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1384	        if (
  1385	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1386	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1387	        ) {
  1388	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1389	            canvas.drawText(
  1390	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1391	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1392	                createBaselineY,
  1393	                accountPromptPaint
  1394	            )
  1395	        }
  1396	        if (
  1397	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1398	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1399	        ) {
  1400	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1401	            canvas.drawText(
  1402	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1403	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1404	                signInBaselineY,
  1405	                formTitlePaint
  1406	            )
  1407	        }
  1408	        if (
  1409	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1410	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1411	        ) {
  1412	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1413	            canvas.drawText(
  1414	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1415	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1416	                forgotBaselineY,
  1417	                accountPromptPaint
  1418	            )
  1419	        }
  1420	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1421	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1422	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1423	    }

  1425	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1426	        canvas: Canvas,
  1427	        stageLeft: Float,
  1428	        stageTop: Float,
  1429	        stageScale: Float,
  1430	        progress: Float,
  1431	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1432	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1433	    ) {
  1434	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1435	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1436	        }
  1437	
  1438	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1439	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1440	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1441	            return
  1442	        }
  1443	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1444	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1445	            return
  1446	        }
  1447	
  1448	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1449	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1450	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1451	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1452	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1453	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1454	
  1455	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1456	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1457	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1458	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1459	
  1460	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1461	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1462	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1463	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1464	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1465	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1466	            canvas,
  1467	            fieldX,
  1468	            rememberMeY,
  1469	            stageLeft,
  1470	            stageTop,
  1471	            stageScale,
  1472	            alpha
  1473	        )
  1474	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1475	            canvas,
  1476	            stageLeft,
  1477	            stageTop,
  1478	            stageScale,
  1479	            alpha,
  1480	            yOffset,
  1481	            focusShiftY
  1482	        )
  1483	
  1484	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1485	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1486	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1487	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1488	    }

  1669	
  1670	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1671	        canvas: Canvas,
  1672	        stageLeft: Float,
  1673	        stageTop: Float,
  1674	        stageScale: Float,
  1675	        alpha: Float,
  1676	        yOffset: Float,
  1677	        focusShiftY: Float
  1678	    ) {
  1679	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1680	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1681	
  1682	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1683	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1684	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1685	
  1686	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1687	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1688	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1689	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1690	
  1691	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1692	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1693	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1694	                canvas,
  1695	                activeDropdown,
  1696	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1697	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1698	                stageLeft,
  1699	                stageTop,
  1700	                stageScale,
  1701	                alpha,
  1702	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1703	            )
  1704	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1705	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1706	            }
  1707	            return
  1708	        }
  1709	
  1710	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1711	        drawMockSignInField(
  1712	            canvas,
  1713	            activeField,
  1714	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1715	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1716	            stageLeft,
  1717	            stageTop,
  1718	            stageScale,
  1719	            alpha,
  1720	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1721	        )
  1722	    }

  1724	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1725	        canvas: Canvas,
  1726	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1727	        stageLeft: Float,
  1728	        stageTop: Float,
  1729	        stageScale: Float,
  1730	        progress: Float,
  1731	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1732	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1733	    ) {
  1734	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1735	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1736	        try {
  1737	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1738	                canvas,
  1739	                stageLeft,
  1740	                stageTop,
  1741	                stageScale,
  1742	                progress,
  1743	                formFocusMotion,
  1744	                updateLabelAnimation
  1745	            )
  1746	        } finally {
  1747	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1748	        }
  1749	    }

  1751	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1752	        canvas: Canvas,
  1753	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1754	        stageLeft: Float,
  1755	        stageTop: Float,
  1756	        stageScale: Float,
  1757	        alpha: Float
  1758	    ) {
  1759	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1760	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1761	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1762	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1763	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1764	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1765	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1766	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1767	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1768	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1769	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1770	
  1771	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1772	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1773	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1774	        canvas.drawText(
  1775	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1776	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1777	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1778	            formTitlePaint
  1779	        )
  1780	    }

  1843	
  1844	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1845	        canvas: Canvas,
  1846	        x: Float,
  1847	        y: Float,
  1848	        stageLeft: Float,
  1849	        stageTop: Float,
  1850	        stageScale: Float,
  1851	        alpha: Float,
  1852	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1853	    ) {
  1854	        tempRect.set(
  1855	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1856	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1857	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1858	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1859	        )
  1860	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1861	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1862	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1863	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1864	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1865	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1866	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1867	            pressed -> 92f
  1868	            enabled -> 68f
  1869	            else -> 18f
  1870	        }
  1871	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1872	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1873	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1874	            pressed -> 238f
  1875	            enabled -> 210f
  1876	            else -> 118f
  1877	        }
  1878	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1879	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1880	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1881	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1882	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1883	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1884	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1885	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1886	        }
  1887	
  1888	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1889	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1890	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1891	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1892	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1893	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1894	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1895	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1896	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1897	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1898	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1899	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1900	            formTitlePaint
  1901	        )
  1902	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1903	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1904	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1905	            enabled -> 255f
  1906	            else -> 138f
  1907	        }).roundToInt()
  1908	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1909	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1910	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1911	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1912	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1913	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1914	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1915	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1916	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1917	            canvas.drawLine(
  1918	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1919	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1920	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1921	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1922	                backChevronPaint
  1923	            )
  1924	            canvas.drawLine(
  1925	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1926	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1927	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1928	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1929	                backChevronPaint
  1930	            )
  1931	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1932	        } else {
  1933	            tempRect.centerX()
  1934	        }
  1935	        canvas.drawText(
  1936	            buttonText,
  1937	            textX,
  1938	            baselineY,
  1939	            formTitlePaint
  1940	        )
  1941	    }
  1942	
  1943	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1944	        canvas: Canvas,
  1945	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1946	        radius: Float,
  1947	        alpha: Float
  1948	    ) {
  1949	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1950	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1951	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1952	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1953	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1954	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1955	            sweepLeft,
  1956	            buttonRect.top,
  1957	            sweepRight,
  1958	            buttonRect.top,
  1959	            intArrayOf(
  1960	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1961	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1962	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1963	            ),
  1964	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1965	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1966	        )
  1967	        canvas.save()
  1968	        tempPath.reset()
  1969	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1970	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1971	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1972	        canvas.restore()
  1973	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1974	    }

  2033	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2034	        canvas: Canvas,
  2035	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2036	        left: Float,
  2037	        top: Float,
  2038	        boxWidth: Float,
  2039	        stageScale: Float,
  2040	        alpha: Float
  2041	    ) {
  2042	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2043	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2044	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2045	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2046	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2047	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2048	            pressed -> 82f
  2049	            selected -> 64f
  2050	            else -> 14f
  2051	        }).roundToInt()
  2052	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2053	            pressed -> 245f
  2054	            selected -> 235f
  2055	            else -> 142f
  2056	        }).roundToInt()
  2057	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2058	
  2059	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2060	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2061	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2062	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2063	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2064	
  2065	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2066	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2067	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2068	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2069	        canvas.drawText(
  2070	            target.displayText,
  2071	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2072	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2073	            formInputPaint
  2074	        )
  2075	    }

  2077	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2078	        canvas: Canvas,
  2079	        text: String,
  2080	        centerX: Float,
  2081	        topY: Float,
  2082	        maxWidth: Float,
  2083	        stageLeft: Float,
  2084	        stageTop: Float,
  2085	        stageScale: Float,
  2086	        alpha: Float
  2087	    ): Float {
  2088	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2089	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2090	            canvas,
  2091	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2092	            centerX,
  2093	            topY,
  2094	            maxWidth,
  2095	            stageLeft,
  2096	            stageTop,
  2097	            stageScale,
  2098	            alpha,
  2099	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2100	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  2101	        )
  2102	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2103	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2104	            canvas,
  2105	            middleText,
  2106	            centerX,
  2107	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2108	            maxWidth,
  2109	            stageLeft,
  2110	            stageTop,
  2111	            stageScale,
  2112	            alpha,
  2113	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2114	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2115	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2116	        )
  2117	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2118	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2119	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2120	            canvas,
  2121	            bottomText,
  2122	            centerX,
  2123	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2124	            maxWidth,
  2125	            stageLeft,
  2126	            stageTop,
  2127	            stageScale,
  2128	            alpha,
  2129	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2130	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2131	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2132	        )
  2133	    }
  2134	
  2135	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2136	        canvas: Canvas,
  2137	        text: String,
  2138	        centerX: Float,
  2139	        topY: Float,
  2140	        maxWidth: Float,
  2141	        stageLeft: Float,
  2142	        stageTop: Float,
  2143	        stageScale: Float,
  2144	        alpha: Float,
  2145	        textSize: Float,
  2146	        lineHeight: Float,
  2147	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2148	    ): Float {
  2149	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2150	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2151	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2152	
  2153	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2154	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2155	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2156	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2157	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2158	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2159	        }
  2160	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2161	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2162	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2163	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2164	        }
  2165	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2166	    }

  2168	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2169	        canvas: Canvas,
  2170	        columnX: Float,
  2171	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2172	        stageLeft: Float,
  2173	        stageTop: Float,
  2174	        stageScale: Float,
  2175	        alpha: Float
  2176	    ) {
  2177	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2178	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  2179	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  2180	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  2181	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2182	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  2183	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  2184	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2185	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2186	        canvas.drawText(
  2187	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  2188	            centerX,
  2189	            baselineY,
  2190	            formLabelPaint
  2191	        )
  2192	
  2193	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  2194	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  2195	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  2196	            avatarLeftX,
  2197	            avatarTopY,
  2198	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2199	            scaledAvatarSize
  2200	        )
  2201	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2202	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2203	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  2204	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2205	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2206	
  2207	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2208	
  2209	        canvas.save()
  2210	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2211	        if (
  2212	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2213	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2214	            dragDirection != null &&
  2215	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2216	        ) {
  2217	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2218	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2219	                canvas,
  2220	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2221	                avatarBounds,
  2222	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  2223	                stageLeft,
  2224	                stageTop,
  2225	                stageScale,
  2226	                alpha
  2227	            )
  2228	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2229	                canvas,
  2230	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  2231	                avatarBounds,
  2232	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  2233	                stageLeft,
  2234	                stageTop,
  2235	                stageScale,
  2236	                alpha
  2237	            )
  2238	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2239	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2240	                canvas,
  2241	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  2242	                avatarBounds,
  2243	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  2244	                stageLeft,
  2245	                stageTop,
  2246	                stageScale,
  2247	                alpha
  2248	            )
  2249	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2250	                canvas,
  2251	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  2252	                avatarBounds,
  2253	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  2254	                stageLeft,
  2255	                stageTop,
  2256	                stageScale,
  2257	                alpha
  2258	            )
  2259	        } else {
  2260	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2261	                canvas,
  2262	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2263	                avatarBounds,
  2264	                0f,
  2265	                stageLeft,
  2266	                stageTop,
  2267	                stageScale,
  2268	                alpha
  2269	            )
  2270	        }
  2271	        canvas.restore()
  2272	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2273	            canvas,
  2274	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2275	            avatarBounds,
  2276	            stageLeft,
  2277	            stageTop,
  2278	            stageScale,
  2279	            alpha
  2280	        )
  2281	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2282	            canvas,
  2283	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2284	            avatarBounds,
  2285	            stageLeft,
  2286	            stageTop,
  2287	            stageScale,
  2288	            alpha
  2289	        )
  2290	    }

  2332	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  2333	        canvas: Canvas,
  2334	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2335	        stageLeft: Float,
  2336	        stageTop: Float,
  2337	        stageScale: Float
  2338	    ) {

  2341	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,

  2349	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2350	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2351	    }

  2353	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2354	        canvas: Canvas,
  2355	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2356	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2357	        stageLeft: Float,
  2358	        stageTop: Float,
  2359	        stageScale: Float,
  2360	        alpha: Float
  2361	    ) {
  2362	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2363	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2364	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2365	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2366	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2367	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2368	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2369	
  2370	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2371	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2372	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2373	        } else {
  2374	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2375	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2376	        }
  2377	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2378	    }

  2380	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2381	        canvas: Canvas,
  2382	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2383	        x: Float,
  2384	        y: Float,
  2385	        stageLeft: Float,
  2386	        stageTop: Float,
  2387	        stageScale: Float,
  2388	        alpha: Float,

  2390	    ) {
