  2392	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2393	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2394	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2395	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2396	        )
  2397	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2398	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2399	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2400	
  2401	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2402	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2403	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2404	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2405	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2406	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2407	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2408	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2409	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2410	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2411	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2412	        } else {
  2413	            0f
  2414	        }
  2415	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2416	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2417	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2418	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2419	                canvas = canvas,
  2420	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2421	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2422	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2423	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2424	                alpha = textAlpha
  2425	            )
  2426	        }
  2427	        canvas.drawText(
  2428	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2429	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2430	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2431	            formInputPaint
  2432	        )
  2433	
  2434	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2435	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2436	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2437	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2438	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2439	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2440	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2441	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2442	    }
  2443	
  2444	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2445	        canvas: Canvas,
  2446	        stageLeft: Float,
  2447	        stageTop: Float,
  2448	        stageScale: Float,
  2449	        alpha: Float
  2450	    ) {
  2451	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2452	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2453	
  2454	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2455	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2456	            dropdown,
  2457	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2458	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2459	        )
  2460	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2461	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2462	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2463	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2464	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2465	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2466	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2467	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2468	        } else {
  2469	            0f
  2470	        }
  2471	
  2472	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2473	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2474	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2475	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2476	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2477	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2478	            } else {
  2479	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2480	            }
  2481	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2482	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2483	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2484	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2485	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2486	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2487	
  2488	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2489	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2490	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2491	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2492	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2493	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2494	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2495	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2496	            } else {
  2497	                0f
  2498	            }
  2499	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2500	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2501	                    canvas = canvas,
  2502	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2503	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2504	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2505	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2506	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2507	                )
  2508	            }
  2509	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2510	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2511	            canvas.drawText(
  2512	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2513	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2514	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2515	                formInputPaint
  2516	            )
  2517	        }
  2518	
  2519	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2520	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2521	                canvas,
  2522	                dropdown,
  2523	                top,
  2524	                right,
  2525	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2526	                stageScale,
  2527	                optionAlpha
  2528	            )
  2529	        }
  2530	    }
  2531	
  2532	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2533	        canvas: Canvas,
  2534	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2535	        left: Float,
  2536	        centerY: Float,
  2537	        iconSize: Float,
  2538	        alpha: Int
  2539	    ) {
  2540	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2541	        val width: Float
  2542	        val height: Float
  2543	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2544	            width = iconSize
  2545	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2546	        } else {
  2547	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2548	            height = iconSize
  2549	        }
  2550	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2551	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2552	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2553	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2554	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2555	    }

  2557	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2558	        canvas: Canvas,
  2559	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2560	        top: Float,
  2561	        right: Float,
  2562	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2563	        stageScale: Float,
  2564	        alpha: Int
  2565	    ) {
  2566	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2567	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2568	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2569	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2570	
  2571	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2572	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2573	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2574	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2575	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2576	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2577	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2578	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2579	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2580	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2581	
  2582	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2583	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2584	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2585	
  2586	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2587	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2588	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2589	    }

  2591	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2592	        canvas: Canvas,
  2593	        field: MockSignInField,
  2594	        x: Float,
  2595	        y: Float,
  2596	        stageLeft: Float,
  2597	        stageTop: Float,
  2598	        stageScale: Float,
  2599	        alpha: Float,
  2600	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2601	    ) {
  2602	        tempRect.set(
  2603	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2604	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2605	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2606	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2607	        )
  2608	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2609	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2610	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2611	
  2612	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2613	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2614	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2615	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2616	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2617	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2618	            labelProgress
  2619	        ) * stageScale
  2620	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2621	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2622	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2623	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2624	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2625	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2626	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2627	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2628	            labelProgress
  2629	        )
  2630	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2631	        canvas.drawText(
  2632	            label,
  2633	            labelX,
  2634	            labelBaselineY,
  2635	            formLabelPaint
  2636	        )
  2637	
  2638	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2639	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2640	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2641	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2642	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2643	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2644	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2645	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2646	
  2647	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2648	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2649	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2650	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2651	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2652	
  2653	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2654	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2655	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2656	                canvas.drawLine(
  2657	                    caretX,
  2658	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2659	                    caretX,
  2660	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2661	                    formCaretPaint
  2662	                )
  2663	            }
  2664	        }
  2665	    }

  2667	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2668	        canvas: Canvas,

  2672	        stageTop: Float,
  2673	        stageScale: Float,

  2680	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2681	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2682	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()

  2689	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2690	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2691

  2693	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2694	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2695	            canvas.drawLine(
  2696	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2697	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,

  2700	                rememberMeCheckPaint

  2705	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2706	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2707	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2708	            )
  2709	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2710	        }
  2711	
  2712	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2713	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2714	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2715	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2716	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2717	        canvas.drawText(
  2718	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2719	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2720	            baselineY,
  2721	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2722	        )
  2723	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2724	    }

  2726	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2727	        canvas: Canvas,
  2728	        centerX: Float,
  2729	        topY: Float,
  2730	        stageLeft: Float,
  2731	        stageTop: Float,
  2732	        stageScale: Float,
  2733	        alpha: Float
  2734	    ) {
  2735	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2736	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2737	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale

  2748	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2749	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2750	    }
  2751	
  2752	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2753	        source: Bitmap,
  2754	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2755	    ): Bitmap {
  2756	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2757	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2758	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2759	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2760	
  2761	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2762	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2763	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2764	        }
  2765	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2766	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2767	        }
  2768	    }
  2769	
  2770	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2771	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2772	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2773	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2774	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2775	
  2776	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2777	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2778	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2779	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2780	
  2781	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2782	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2783	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2784	            val dx = x - centerX
  2785	            val dy = y - centerY
  2786	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2787	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2788	            } else {
  2789	                pixels[index]
  2790	            }
  2791	        }
  2792	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2793	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2794	        }
  2795	    }
  2796	
  2797	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2798	        pixels: IntArray,
  2799	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2800	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2801	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2802	    ): BooleanArray {
  2803	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2804	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2805	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2806	        }
  2807	
  2808	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2809	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2810	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2811	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2812	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2813	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2814	
  2815	                mask[index] = true
  2816	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2817	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2818	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2819	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2820	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2821	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2822	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2823	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2824	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2825	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2826	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2827	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2828	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2829	                        )
  2830	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2831	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2832	                        }
  2833	                    }
  2834	                }
  2835	            }
  2836	        }
  2837	        return mask
  2838	    }
  2839	
  2840	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2841	        pixel: Int,
  2842	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2843	    ): Boolean {
  2844	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2845	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2846	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2847	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2848	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2849	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2850	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2851	
  2852	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2853	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2854	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2855	    }
  2856	
  2857	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2858	        return when {
  2859	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2860	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2861	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2862	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2863	            else -> null
  2864	        }
  2865	    }
  2866	
  2867	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2868	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2869	    }
  2870	
  2871	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  2872	        return IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis > 0L &&
  2873	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  2874	    }
  2875	
  2876	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2877	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2878	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2879	
  2880	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2881	
  2882	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2883	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2884	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2885	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  2886	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  2887	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  2888	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  2889	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  2890	                ) * stage.scale
  2891	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  2892	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2893	            } else {
  2894	                null
  2895	            }
  2896	        }
  2897	
  2898	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2899	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2900	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2901	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2902	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2903	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2904	            val dx = x - centerX
  2905	            val dy = y - centerY
  2906	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2907	        }?.profile
  2908	    }
  2909	
  2910	    private fun settledCreateAccountPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2911	        if (!accountPromptCanOpen(MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount)) return false
  2912	
  2913	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2914	        if (portraitIntroAmount() >= 0.999f) {
  2915	            return portraitPromptHit(
  2916	                x,
  2917	                y,
  2918	                portraitPromptCreateCenterY(),
  2919	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH
  2920	            )
  2921	        }
  2922	        val centerX = stage.left + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stage.scale
  2923	        val centerY = accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale) +
  2924	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  2925	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2926	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  2927	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  2928	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2929	    }

  2931	    private fun settledSignInPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {

  3109	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3110	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3111	
  3112	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3113	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3114	        if (
  3115	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3116	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3117	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3118	        ) {
  3119	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3120	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3121	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3122	            } else {
  3123	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3124	            }
  3125	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3126	        }
  3127	
  3128	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3129	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3130	        }
  3131	        return true
  3132	    }

  3191	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3192	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3193	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3194	
  3195	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3196	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3197	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3198	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3199	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3200	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3201	        }
  3202	
  3203	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3204	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3205	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3206	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3207	        }
  3208	        return true
  3209	    }

  3385	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(

  3387	        pointerY: Float,
  3388	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3389	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3390	        stage: StageMetrics
  3391	    ): Boolean {
  3392	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  3393	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  3394	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3395	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  3396	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  3397	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  3398	    }
  3399	
  3400	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  3401	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  3402	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  3403	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3404	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3405	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3406	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  3407	            activeComposingText = ""
  3408	            focusedMockField = field
  3409	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3410	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3411	        }
  3412	        requestFocus()
  3413	        post {
  3414	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3415	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  3416	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  3417	        }
  3418	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3419	    }
  3420	
  3421	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {
  3422	        if (focusedMockField == null) return
  3423	        activeComposingText = ""
  3424	        focusedMockField = null
  3425	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  3426	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3427	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3428	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3429	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3430	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3431	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3432	        clearFocus()
  3433	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3434	    }

  3480	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  3481	        left: Float,
  3482	        width: Float,
  3483	        labelWidth: Float,
  3484	        boxWidth: Float
  3485	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  3486	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3487	        val labelX = left + gap
  3488	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  3489	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  3490	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  3491	            labelX = labelX,
  3492	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  3493	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  3494	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  3495	        )
  3496	    }

  3498	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(

  3500	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  3501	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  3502	    ): Float {
  3503	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  3504	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3505	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  3506	    }

  3659	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3660	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  3661	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  3662	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  3663	        } else {
  3664	            optionCount
  3665	        }
  3666	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  3667	    }

  3669	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  3670	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3671	    }
  3672	
  3673	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3674	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3675	    }
  3676	
  3677	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3678	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3679	    }
  3680	
  3681	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  3682	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3683	    }
  3684	
  3685	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  3686	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3687	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3688	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  3689	    }
  3690	
  3691	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  3692	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3693	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  3694	            return
  3695	        }
  3696	
  3697	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  3698	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  3699	    }

  3768	    private fun setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget) {

  3776	        resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3777	        activeComposingText = ""

  3779	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3780	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  3781	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3782	        } else {
  3783	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3784	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3785	            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3786	            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3787	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3788	        }

  3795	    private fun mockForgotPasswordHelpText(): String {

  3798	            MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> forgotRecoveryTarget.sentHelpText
  3799	            MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle -> {
  3800	                if (emailText.trim().isEmpty()) {
  3801	                    MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_EMPTY_HELP_TEXT
  3802	                } else {

  3812	            MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> forgotRecoveryTarget.sentButtonText
  3813	            MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle -> forgotRecoveryTarget.submitButtonText
  3814	        }

  3817	    private fun mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit(): Boolean {
  3818	        return activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  3819	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle &&
  3820	            emailText.trim().isNotEmpty()
  3821	    }
  3822	
  3823	    private fun startMockForgotPasswordSubmission() {
  3824	        if (!mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()) {
  3825	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3826	            return
  3827	        }
  3828	
  3829	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3830	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  3831	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3832	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3833	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3834	    }
  3835	
  3836	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3837	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3838	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3839	        ) {
  3840	            1f
  3841	        } else {
  3842	            0f
  3843	        }
  3844	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3845	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3846	    }
  3847	
  3848	    private fun isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating(): Boolean {
  3849	        return forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  3850	    }
  3851	
  3852	    private fun finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded() {
  3853	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  3854	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3855	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3856	            return
  3857	        }
  3858	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3859	
  3860	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3861	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3862	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3863	    }
  3864	
  3865	    private fun resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission() {
  3866	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  3867	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
  3868	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3869	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3870	    }
  3871	
  3872	    private fun startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  3873	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3874	        startProgress: Float = 0f
  3875	    ) {
  3876	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount || isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) return
  3877	
  3878	        val nextIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction)
  3879	        if (nextIndex == mockCreateAvatarIndex) return
  3880

  4128	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  4129	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  4130	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  4131	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  4132	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  4133	        }
  4134	
  4135	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  4136	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  4137	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  4138	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  4139	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  4140	    }

  4251	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4252	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4253	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4254	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  4255	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4256	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4257	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4258	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  4259	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  4260	        activeComposingText = ""
  4261	        return true
  4262	    }
  4263	
  4264	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4265	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4266	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4267	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4268	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4269	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4270	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  4271	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  4272	        activeComposingText = composingText
  4273	        return true
  4274	    }

  4281	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  4282	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4283	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4284	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  4285	            activeComposingText = ""
  4286	            return true
  4287	        }
  4288	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  4289	        activeComposingText = ""
  4290	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  4291	        return true
  4292	    }

  4294	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4295	        activeComposingText = ""
  4296	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  4297	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  4298	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  4299	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  4300	        } else if (
  4301	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  4302	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  4303	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  4304	        ) {
  4305	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4306	        } else {
  4307	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4308	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4309	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4310	        }
  4311	        return true
  4312	    }

  4314	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4315	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4316	        return when (keyCode) {
  4317	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4318	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4319	            else -> {
  4320	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4321	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  4322	            }
  4323	        }
  4324	    }
  4325	
  4326	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  4327	        return buildString {
  4328	            input.forEach { character ->
  4329	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  4330	                    append(character)
  4331	                }
  4332	            }
  4333	        }
  4334	    }
  4335	
  4336	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  4337	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  4338	
  4339	        var startIndex = 0
  4340	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  4341	            startIndex += 1
  4342	        }
  4343	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  4344	    }
  4345	
  4346	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  4347	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  4348	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  4349	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  4350	                lines += ""
  4351	            }
  4352	
  4353	            var currentLine = ""
  4354	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  4355	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  4356	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  4357	                .forEach { word ->
  4358	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  4359	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  4360	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  4361	                    } else {
  4362	                        lines += currentLine
  4363	                        currentLine = word
  4364	                    }
  4365	                }
  4366	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  4367	                lines += currentLine
  4368	            }
  4369	        }
  4370	        return lines
  4371	    }
  4372	
  4373	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  4374	        return when {
  4375	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  4376	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  4377	            else -> current
  4378	        }
  4379	    }
  4380	
  4381	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  4382	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  4383	
  4384	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  4385	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  4386	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  4387	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  4388	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  4389	        return StageMetrics(
  4390	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  4391	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  4392	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  4393	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  4394	            scale = stageScale
  4395	        )
  4396	    }
  4397	
  4398	    private fun isPortraitIntroLayout(): Boolean = width <= height
  4399	
  4400	    private fun portraitIntroAmount(): Float {
  4401	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4402	        return 1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4403	    }
  4404	
  4405	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  4406	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f
  4407	
  4408	        val widthScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  4409	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return widthScale
  4410	
  4411	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  4412	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  4413	    }
  4414	
  4415	    private fun portraitSafeTop(): Float = systemBarInsetTopPx / portraitDesignScale()
  4416	
  4417	    private fun portraitSafeBottom(): Float {
  4418	        return (height - systemBarInsetBottomPx) / portraitDesignScale()
  4419	    }
  4420	
  4421	    private fun portraitStackCenter(fraction: Float): Float {
  4422	        val top = portraitSafeTop()
  4423	        return top + (portraitSafeBottom() - top) * fraction
  4424	    }
  4425	
  4426	    private fun portraitLogoTop(): Float {
  4427	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  4428	        return portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f + portraitContentOffsetY() -
  4429	            portraitOuterBreathingRoom() / 2f
  4430	    }
  4431	
  4432	    private fun portraitAvatarSize(): Float = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  4433	
  4434	    private fun portraitProfileNameHeight(): Float = 72f * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  4435	
  4436	    private fun portraitBaseAvatarTop(): Float {
  4437	        val stackHeight = portraitAvatarSize() + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_NAME_GAP + portraitProfileNameHeight()
  4438	        val responsiveShift = lerpFloat(
  4439	            PORTRAIT_COMPACT_AVATAR_SHIFT_Y,
  4440	            PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_AVATAR_SHIFT_Y,
  4441	            portraitVerticalRoomProgress()
  4442	        )
  4443	        return portraitStackCenter(0.5f) - stackHeight / 2f + responsiveShift + portraitContentOffsetY()
  4444	    }
  4445	
  4446	    private fun portraitAvatarShiftY(): Float {
  4447	        val logoVisibleBottom = portraitLogoTop() +
  4448	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE * PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_BOTTOM_FRACTION
  4449	        val upperGap = (portraitBaseAvatarTop() - logoVisibleBottom).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4450	        return -upperGap * (1f - PORTRAIT_INTER_STACK_GAP_SCALE)
  4451	    }
  4452	
  4453	    private fun portraitAvatarTop(): Float = portraitBaseAvatarTop() + portraitAvatarShiftY()
  4454	
  4455	    private fun portraitProfileNameTop(): Float {
  4456	        return portraitAvatarTop() + portraitAvatarSize() + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_NAME_GAP
  4457	    }
  4458	
  4459	    private fun portraitBasePromptStackCenterY(): Float {
  4460	        return portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitContentOffsetY() +
  4461	            portraitOuterBreathingRoom() / 2f
  4462	    }
  4463	
  4464	    private fun portraitLinkStackShiftY(): Float {
  4465	        val baseNameVisibleBottom = portraitBaseAvatarTop() + portraitAvatarSize() +
  4466	            PORTRAIT_PROFILE_NAME_GAP +
  4467	            portraitProfileNameHeight() * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_NAME_VISIBLE_BOTTOM_FRACTION
  4468	        val baseLinkVisibleTop = portraitBasePromptStackCenterY() -
  4469	            portraitPromptCenterSpacing() - PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT
  4470	        val lowerGap = (baseLinkVisibleTop - baseNameVisibleBottom).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4471	        return portraitAvatarShiftY() - lowerGap * (1f - PORTRAIT_INTER_STACK_GAP_SCALE)
  4472	    }
  4473	
  4474	    private fun portraitPromptCreateCenterY(): Float {
  4475	        return portraitBasePromptStackCenterY() - portraitPromptCenterSpacing() +
  4476	            portraitLinkStackShiftY() + portraitLinkBottomSafetyShiftY()
  4477	    }
  4478	
  4479	    private fun portraitLinkBottomSafetyShiftY(): Float {
  4480	        val createCenter = portraitBasePromptStackCenterY() - portraitPromptCenterSpacing() +
  4481	            portraitLinkStackShiftY()
  4482	        val forgotVisibleBottom = createCenter +
  4483	            portraitPromptCenterSpacing() * PORTRAIT_LINK_SPACING_SCALE * 2f +
  4484	            PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT
  4485	        val safeBottom = portraitSafeBottom() - PORTRAIT_LINK_BOTTOM_MARGIN
  4486	        return (safeBottom - forgotVisibleBottom).coerceAtMost(0f)
  4487	    }
  4488	
  4489	    private fun portraitPromptSignInCenterY(): Float {
  4490	        return portraitPromptCreateCenterY() +
  4491	            portraitPromptCenterSpacing() * PORTRAIT_LINK_SPACING_SCALE
  4492	    }
  4493	
  4494	    private fun portraitPromptForgotCenterY(): Float {
  4495	        return portraitPromptCreateCenterY() +
  4496	            portraitPromptCenterSpacing() * PORTRAIT_LINK_SPACING_SCALE * 2f
  4497	    }
  4498	
  4499	    private fun portraitContentOffsetY(): Float {
  4500	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  4501	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f
  4502	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()
  4503	        val topBreathingRoom = rawLogoTop + PORTRAIT_LOGO_WHITE_EYE_TOP_INSET - portraitSafeTop()
  4504	        val bottomBreathingRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  4505	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  4506	        return (bottomBreathingRoom - topBreathingRoom) / 2f
  4507	    }
  4508	
  4509	    private fun portraitOuterBreathingRoom(): Float {
  4510	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  4511	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f
  4512	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()
  4513	        val rawTopRoom = rawLogoTop + PORTRAIT_LOGO_WHITE_EYE_TOP_INSET - portraitSafeTop()
  4514	        val rawBottomRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  4515	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  4516	        return (rawTopRoom + rawBottomRoom) / 2f
  4517	    }
  4518	
  4519	    private fun portraitVerticalRoomProgress(): Float {
  4520	        val usableHeight = portraitSafeBottom() - portraitSafeTop()
  4521	        return ((usableHeight - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT) /
  4522	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))
  4523	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4524	    }
  4525	
  4526	    private fun portraitPromptCenterSpacing(): Float {
  4527	        return lerpFloat(
  4528	            PORTRAIT_COMPACT_PROMPT_CENTER_SPACING,
  4529	            PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_PROMPT_CENTER_SPACING,
  4530	            portraitVerticalRoomProgress()
  4531	        )
  4532	    }
  4533	
  4534	    private fun mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  4535	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  4536	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4537	        return MOCK_AUTH_OPEN_LANDSCAPE_STAGE_LIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  4538	    }
  4539	
  4540	    private fun signInStackShiftY(): Float {
  4541	        return if (width > height) {
  4542	            SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  4543	        } else {
  4544	            SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  4545	        }
  4546	    }
  4547	
  4548	    private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(): Float {
  4549	        return if (width > height) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  4550	    }
  4551	
  4552	    private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(): Float {
  4553	        return if (width > height) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  4554	    }
  4555	
  4556	    private fun accountPromptCreateCenterX(): Float {
  4557	        return mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  4558	    }
  4559	
  4560	    private fun accountPromptForgotCenterX(): Float {
  4561	        return (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_USABLE_WIDTH) / 2f +
  4562	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_USABLE_WIDTH * 0.8f
  4563	    }

  4565	    private fun currentIntroSnapshot(): CinerificIntroSnapshot {
  4566	        val activeFlowName = activeMockFlow
  4567	            ?.takeIf { mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress > 0f }
  4568	            ?.name
  4569	            .orEmpty()
  4570	        return CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  4571	            activeFlowName = activeFlowName,
  4572	            usernameText = usernameText,
  4573	            passwordText = passwordText,
  4574	            confirmPasswordText = confirmPasswordText,
  4575	            emailText = emailText,
  4576	            monthText = monthText,
  4577	            dayText = dayText,
  4578	            yearText = yearText,
  4579	            subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText),
  4580	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = forgotRecoveryTarget.name,
  4581	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = forgotPasswordSubmissionState.name,
  4582	            rememberMeChecked = rememberMeChecked,
  4583	            createAvatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  4584	            portraitProfileIndex = portraitProfileIndex
  4585	        )
  4586	    }
  4587	
  4588	    private fun notifyIntroSnapshotChanged() {
  4589	        val snapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
  4590	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
  4591	
  4592	        appliedIntroSnapshot = snapshot
  4593	        onIntroSnapshotChanged?.invoke(snapshot)
  4594	    }

  4596	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  4597	        if (
  4598	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4599	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  4600	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4601	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  4602	        ) {
  4603	            return
  4604	        }
  4605	
  4606	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  4607	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4608	        activeComposingText = ""
  4609	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  4610	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4611	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4612	        pressedMockField = null
  4613	        pressedRememberMe = false
  4614	        pressedBackChevron = false
  4615	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4616	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  4617	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  4618	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  4619	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  4620	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  4621	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4622	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4623	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4624	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4625	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4626	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  4627	            emailText = ""
  4628	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4629	        }
  4630	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  4631	        activeMockFlow = flow
  4632	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4633	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4634	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4635	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4636	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4637	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4638	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4639	    }
  4640	
  4641	    private fun openMockSignInScreen() {
  4642	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 1f) return
  4643	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  4644	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  4645	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4646	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  4647	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4648	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4649	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4650	    }
  4651

  4653	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 1f) return
  4654	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  4655	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  4656	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4657	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  4658	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4659	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()

  4661	    }

  4665	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  4666	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  4667	        forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  4668	        emailText = ""

  4670	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4671	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress

  4677	    private fun closeMockSignInScreen() {
  4678	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) return
  4679	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  4680	        pressedMockField = null
  4681	        pressedRememberMe = false
  4682	        pressedBackChevron = false
  4683	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4684	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  4685	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4686	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4687	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4688	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4689	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4690	        activeComposingText = ""
  4691	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  4692	        usernameText = ""
  4693	        passwordText = ""
  4694	        confirmPasswordText = ""
  4695	        emailText = ""
  4696	        monthText = MOCK_FORM_MONTH_TEXT
  4697	        dayText = MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT
  4698	        yearText = MOCK_FORM_YEAR_TEXT
  4699	        subscriptionTierText = MOCK_FORM_SUBSCRIPTION_TIER_TEXT
  4700	        forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  4701	        resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4702	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4703	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  4704	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  4705	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  4706	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
  4707	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
  4708	        }
  4709	        focusedMockField = null
  4710	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4711	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4712	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4713	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4714	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4715	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4716	        clearFocus()
  4717	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4718	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  4719	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4720	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4721	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4722	    }
  4723	
  4724	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  4725	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  4726	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4727	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4728	        return lerpFloat(
  4729	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  4730	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  4731	            progress
  4732	        )
  4733	    }
  4734	
  4735	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  4736	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  4737	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  4738	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4739	    }
  4740	
  4741	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  4742	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  4743	    }
  4744	
  4745	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  4746	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  4747	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  4748	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  4749	    }
  4750	
  4751	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  4752	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  4753	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  4754	    }
  4755	
  4756	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  4757	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  4758	    }
  4759	
  4760	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  4761	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  4762	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  4763	            .roundToInt()
  4764	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  4765	            .toLong()
  4766	    }
  4767	
  4768	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4769	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4770	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4771	
  4772	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4773	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4774	            activeMockFlow = null
  4775	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4776	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4777	            pressedMockField = null
  4778	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4779	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4780	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4781	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4782	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4783	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4784	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4785	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4786	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4787	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  4788	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4789	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4790	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4791	            return
  4792	        }
  4793	
  4794	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  4795	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4796	    }
  4797	
  4798	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  4799	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4800	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4801	        }
  4802	
  4803	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4804	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4805	        }
  4806	
  4807	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  4808	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  4809	        }
  4810	
  4811	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4812	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  4813	        }
  4814	
  4815	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4816	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  4817	        }
  4818	
  4819	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4820	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  4821	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  4822	        }
  4823	
  4824	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4825	            return ""
  4826	        }
  4827	
  4828	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4829	            return ""
  4830	        }
  4831	
  4832	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4833	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  4834	        }
  4835	
  4836	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4837	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4838	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4839	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4840	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4841	                else -> {
  4842	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4843	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4844	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4845	                    } else {
  4846	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4847	                    }
  4848	                }
  4849	            }
  4850	        }
  4851	    }
  4852	}

  4864	    val forgotRecoveryTargetName: String = "Password",

  4866	    val rememberMeChecked: Boolean = false,

  4902	) {

  4990	private data class AvatarTarget(
  4991	    val profile: CinerificProfile,
  4992	    val bounds: Bounds
  4993	)
  4994	
  4995	private data class StageMetrics(
  4996	    val left: Float,
  4997	    val top: Float,
  4998	    val scale: Float
  4999	)
  5000	
  5001	private data class Bounds(
  5002	    val x: Float,
  5003	    val y: Float,
  5004	    val w: Float,
  5005	    val h: Float
  5006	)
  5007	
  5008	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  5009	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  5010	}
  5011	
  5012	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  5013	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  5014	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5015	}
  5016	
  5017	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  5018	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  5019	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5020	}
  5021	
  5022	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  5023	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  5024	}
  5025	
  5026	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  5027	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  5028	}
  5029	
  5030	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  5031	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5032	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  5033	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  5034	    return base + bounce
  5035	}
  5036	
  5037	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  5038	
  5039	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  5040	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  5041	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  5042	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  5043	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  5044	)
  5045	
  5046	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  5047	    x = bounds.x,
  5048	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  5049	    w = bounds.w,
  5050	    h = bounds.h
  5051	)
  5052	
  5053	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  5054	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5055	    return Color.argb(
  5056	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  5057	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  5058	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  5059	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  5060	    )
  5061	}
  5062	
  5063	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  5064	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  5065	}
  5066	
  5067	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  5068	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  5069	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  5070	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  5071	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  5072	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  5073	    return output
  5074	}
  5075	
  5076	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  5077	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  5078	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  5079	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  5080	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  5081	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  5082	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  5083	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  5084	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  5085	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  5086	    return output
  5087	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and the repository-owned rounded tablet bezel before
    41	opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.
